enumerate(assumptions_headers, 1):\n    ws.cell(row=row, column=i, value=h)\napply_header_style(ws, row, 2)\n\n# Assumptions data\nassumptions = [\n    ('WACC (Weighted Average Cost of Capital)', 0.12),\n    ('Terminal Growth Rate', 0.025),\n    ('Terminal EV/EBITDA Multiple', 15.0),\n    ('Risk-Free Rate (10Y Treasury)', 0.045),\n    ('Equity Risk Premium', 0.06),\n    ('Beta', 1.65),\n    ('Cost of Equity (CAPM)', None),  # Calculated\n    ('Cost of Debt (After-Tax)', None),  # Calculated\n    ('Debt/Total Capital', None),  # Calculated\n    ('Tax Rate', 0.21),\n]\n\n# Calculate derived assumptions\nrisk_free = 0.045\nerp = 0.06\nbeta = 1.65\ncost_of_equity = risk_free + beta * erp  # 4.5% + 1.65 * 6% = 14.4%\n\n# Get latest debt and market cap for WACC components\nlatest_debt = total_debt.get(2026, total_debt.get(2025, 0))\nlatest_equity = total_equity.get(2026, total_equity.get(2025, 0))\nlatest_cash = total_cash.get(2026, total_cash.get(2025, 0))\nev = market_cap + latest_debt - latest_cash\ntotal_capital = market_cap + latest_debt\ndebt_ratio = latest_debt / total_capital if total_capital > 0 else 0\nequity_ratio = market_cap / total_capital if total_capital > 0 else 0\ntax_rate = 0.21\ncost_of_debt_before_tax = 0.05  # Assumed\ncost_of_debt_after_tax = cost_of_debt_before_tax * (1 - tax_rate)\n\nwacc = (equity_ratio * cost_of_equity) + (debt_ratio * cost_of_debt_after_tax)\n\nassumptions[6] = ('Cost of Equity (CAPM: Rf + β × ERP)', cost_of_equity)\nassumptions[7] = ('Cost of Debt (After-Tax)', cost_of_debt_after_tax)\nassumptions[8] = ('Debt/Total Capital Ratio', debt_ratio)\n\nfor i, (label, value) in enumerate(assumptions):\n    r = row + 1 + i\n    ws.cell(row=r, column=1, value=label).font = label_font\n    ws.cell(row=r, column=1).border = thin_border\n    \n    if value is not None:\n        cell = ws.cell(row=r, column=2, value=value)\n        cell.font = number_font\n        cell.border = thin_border\n        if isinstance(value, float) and value < 1:\n            cell.number_format = pct_format\n        elif isinstance(value, float) and value >= 1:\n            cell.number_format = '0.00'\n    else:\n        ws.cell(row=r, column=2, value='N/A').font = number_font\n\n# ============================================================\n# SECTION 2: <= 2025]  # Historical: 2023, 2024, 2025\nprojection_years = list(range(2026, 2033))  # Projections: 2026-2032\n\nheaders = ['($ in millions)', '2023A', '2024A', '2025A', '2026E', '2027E', '2028E', = currency_format\n    cell.border = thin_border\n# Projections (calculate CAGR)\nif len(years) >= 2:\n    cagr = (revenue[years[-1]] / revenue[years[0]]) ** (1 / (years[-1] - years[0])) - 1\nelse:\n    cagr = 0.20  # Default 20%\n\nproj_rev = [revenue[years[-1]] * (1 + cagr) ** (i + 1) for i in range(len(projection_years))]\nfor i, val in enumerate(proj_rev):\n    cell = ws.cell(row=row+1,    cell.border = thin_border\ncogs_margin = cogs[years[-1]] / revenue[years[-1]] if revenue[years[-1]] > 0 else 0.5\nproj_cogs = [r * (1 - cogs_margin) for r in proj_rev]\nfor i, val in enumerate(proj_cogs):\n    cell = ws.cell(row=row+2, column=2+i, value=val)\n    cell.font = number_font\n    cell.number_format = currency_format\n    cell.border = thin_border\n\n# Gross proj_cogs[i] for i in range(len(proj_rev))]\nfor i, val in enumerate(proj_gp):\n    cell = ws.cell(row=row+3, column=2+i, value=val)\n    cell.font = Font(name='Calibri', bold=True, size=11)\n    cell.number_format = currency_format\n    cell.border = thin_border\n\n# Gross Margin %\nws.cell(row=row+4, column=1, value='Gross Margin %').font = pct_font\nfor i, year in enumerate(years):\n cell.border = thin_border\nop_margin = op_income[years[-1]] / revenue[years[-1]] if revenue[years[-1]] > 0 else 0.3\nproj_ebit = [r * op_margin for r in proj_rev]\nfor i, val in for i, fcf in enumerate(proj_fcf_accurate):\n        t = i + 0.5  # mid-year\n        pv_fcf += fcf / ((1 + wacc_used) ** t)\n    \n    # Terminal Value (exit multiple)\n    last_ebitda =
